Uint8Array(buf))).replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/, ""); const unb64u = (s) => Uint8Array.from(atob(s.replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/") + "=".repeat((4 - (s.length % 4)) % 4)), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0)); const cat = (...a) => { const o = new Uint8Array(a.reduce((n, x) => n + x.length, 0)); let i = 0; for (const x of a) { o.set(x, i); i += x.length; } return o; }; const enc = new TextEncoder(); const etDate = (d = new Date()) => new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-CA", { timeZone: "America/New_York" }).format(d).replaceAll("-", ""); const players = () => CONFIG.PLAYERS.split(",").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean); const getDb = async (env) => (await env.KV.get("db", "json")) || { subs: {}, games: {}, picks: {} }; const saveDb = (env, db) => env.KV.put("db", JSON.stringify(db)); // ---------- Web Push (RFC 8291 aes128gcm + VAPID), no libraries ---------- async function getVapid(env) { let v = await env.KV.get("vapid", "json"); if (!v) { const kp = await crypto.subtle.generateKey({ name: "ECDSA", namedCurve: "P-256" }, true, ["sign", "verify"]); v = { pub: b64u(await crypto.subtle.exportKey("raw", kp.publicKey)), priv: await crypto.subtle.exportKey("jwk", kp.privateKey) }; await env.KV.put("vapid", JSON.stringify(v)); } return v; } async function hkdf(ikm, salt, info, len) { const k = await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", ikm, "HKDF", false, ["deriveBits"]); return new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.deriveBits({ name: "HKDF", hash: "SHA-256", salt, info }, k, len * 8)); } async function encryptPayload(sub, text) { const uaPub = unb64u(sub.keys.p256dh), auth = unb64u(sub.keys.auth); const eph = await crypto.subtle.generateKey({ name: "ECDH", namedCurve: "P-256" }, true, ["deriveBits"]); const asPub = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.exportKey("raw", eph.publicKey)); const uaKey = await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", uaPub, { name: "ECDH", namedCurve: "P-256" }, false, []); const secret = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.deriveBits({ name: "ECDH", public: uaKey }, eph.privateKey, 256)); const ikm = await hkdf(secret, auth, cat(enc.encode("WebPush: info\0"), uaPub, asPub), 32); const salt = crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16)); const cek = await hkdf(ikm, salt, enc.encode("Content-Encoding: aes128gcm\0"), 16); const nonce = await hkdf(ikm, salt, enc.encode("Content-Encoding: nonce\0"), 12); const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey("raw", cek, "AES-GCM", false, ["encrypt"]); const ct = new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.encrypt({ name: "AES-GCM", iv: nonce }, key, cat(enc.encode(text), new Uint8Array([2])))); return cat(salt, new Uint8Array([0, 0, 16, 0, 65]), asPub, ct); } async function vapidAuth(env, endpoint) { const v = await getVapid(env); const head = b64u(enc.encode(JSON.stringify({ typ: "JWT", alg: "ES256" }))); const body = b64u(enc.encode(JSON.stringify({ aud: new URL(endpoint).origin, exp: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 12 * 3600, sub: CONFIG.SUBJECT }))); const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey("jwk", v.priv, { name: "ECDSA", namedCurve: "P-256" }, false, ["sign"]); const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign({ name: "ECDSA", hash: "SHA-256" }, key, enc.encode(head + "." + body)); return `vapid t=${head}.${body}.${b64u(sig)}, k=${v.pub}`; } async function push(env, db, name, payload) { const sub = db.subs[name]; if (!sub) return "no subscription"; const r = await fetch(sub.endpoint, { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Encoding": "aes128gcm", "Content-Type": "application/octet-stream", TTL: "43200", Urgency: "high", Authorization: await vapidAuth(env, sub.endpoint) }, body: await encryptPayload(sub, JSON.stringify(payload)), }); if (r.status === 404 || r.status === 410) delete db.subs[name]; return r.status + (r.ok ? "" : " " + (await r.text()).slice(0, 200)); } // ---------- NBA data ---------- // Underdog: odds favourite flag, then spread text, then worse record; ties go to the away team. function oddsOf(c) { const o = c.odds?.[0]; if (!o) return null; const num = (x) => (typeof x === "number" ? x : null); const r = { line: o.details || "", ou: num(o.overUnder), hml: num(o.homeTeamOdds?.moneyLine ?? o.homeMoneyLine), aml: num(o.awayTeamOdds?.moneyLine ?? o.awayMoneyLine) }; return r.line || r.ou !== null || r.hml !== null ? r : null; } function underdogOf(c, h, a) { const o = c.odds?.[0]; const hA = h.team.abbreviation, aA = a.team.abbreviation; if (o?.homeTeamOdds?.favorite === true) return aA; if (o?.awayTeamOdds?.favorite === true) return hA; const hm = o?.homeTeamOdds?.moneyLine, am = o?.awayTeamOdds?.moneyLine; if (typeof hm === "number" && typeof am === "number" && hm !== am) return hm > am ? hA : aA; const m = /^([A-Z]{2,4})\s+-\d/.exec(o?.details || ""); if (m && m[1] === hA) return aA; if (m && m[1] === aA) return hA; const pct = (x) => { const s = (x.records || []).find((r) => r.type === "total")?.summary || x.records?.[0]?.summary || ""; const k = /^(\d+)-(\d+)/.exec(s); return k && +k[1] + +k[2] ? +k[1] / (+k[1] + +k[2]) : 0.5; }; return pct(h) < pct(a) ? hA : aA; } function put(db, g) { const old = db.games[g.id]; if (old?.underdog && (Date.parse(old.start) <= Date.now() || !g.underdog)) g.underdog = old.underdog; if (old?.odds && (Date.parse(old.start) <= Date.now() || !g.odds)) g.odds = old.odds; if (old?.sb) { g.sb = old.sb; g.underdog = old.sb.underdog; } if (old?.tab) { g.tab = old.tab; g.underdog = old.tab.underdog; } db.games[g.id] = g; } async function fetchGames(date) { const r = await fetch(`https://site.api.espn.com/apis/site/v2/sports/basketball/nba/scoreboard?dates=${date}`); const j = await r.json(); return (j.events || []).map((e) => { const c = e.competitions[0]; const h = c.competitors.find((x) => x.homeAway === "home"); const a = c.competitors.find((x) => x.homeAway === "away"); const w = c.competitors.find((x) => x.winner); const done = !!e.status?.type?.completed; const underdog = underdogOf(c, h, a); return { id: e.id, underdog, odds: oddsOf(c), date, start: e.date, home: h.team.abbreviation, away: a.team.abbreviation, homeName: h.team.displayName, awayName: a.team.displayName, done, winner: done && w ? w.team.abbreviation : null }; }); } async function refresh(db, extraDates = []) { const dates = new Set(extraDates); for (const g of Object.values(db.games)) if (!g.done) dates.add(g.date); for (const d of [...dates].sort().slice(-4)) for (const g of await fetchGames(d)) put(db, g); } const upcoming = (db) => Object.values(db.games).filter((g) => Date.parse(g.start) > Date.now()).sort((a, b) => a.start.localeCompare(b.start)); // If nothing upcoming is stored, look ahead up to 10 days for the next game day. async function ensureUpcoming(db) { if (upcoming(db).length) return; for (let i = 0; i <= 10; i++) { const gs = await fetchGames(etDate(new Date(Date.now() + i * 864e5))); for (const g of gs) put(db, g); if (upcoming(db).length) return; } } // Sportsbet head-to-head prices via The Odds API: 1 credit per fetch, at most every 12 hours. const nick = (s) => (s || "").trim().split(/\s+/).pop().toLowerCase(); async function applySportsbet(db) { if (!CONFIG.ODDS_API_KEY) return; if (!db.sbAt || Date.now() - db.sbAt > 12 * 3600e3) { db.sbAt = Date.now(); try { const r = await fetch(`https://api.the-odds-api.com/v4/sports/basketball_nba/odds?bookmakers=sportsbet&markets=h2h&oddsFormat=decimal&apiKey=${CONFIG.ODDS_API_KEY}`); db.sbLeft = r.headers.get("x-requests-remaining"); if (r.ok) { db.sbEvents = (await r.json()).map((e) => { const m = e.bookmakers?.[0]?.markets?.find((x) => x.key === "h2h"); const price = (team) => m?.outcomes?.find((o) => o.name === team)?.price; return { home: nick(e.home_team), away: nick(e.away_team), h: price(e.home_team), a: price(e.away_team) }; }).filter((x) => x.h && x.a); } } catch (e) {} } for (const g of upcoming(db)) { const ev = (db.sbEvents || []).find((x) => x.home === nick(g.homeName) && x.away === nick(g.awayName)); if (!ev) continue; g.sb = { h: ev.h, a: ev.a, underdog: ev.h === ev.a ? g.underdog : ev.h > ev.a ? g.home : g.away }; g.underdog = g.sb.underdog; } } // TAB head-to-head prices from ESPN Australia's schedule page (free, no API key). const TAB_HEADERS = { "user-agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/124.0 Safari/537.36", "accept-language": "en-AU,en;q=0.9", accept: "text/html,application/xhtml+xml" }; function parseTab(html) { const text = html.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, " ").replace(/$|$/g, "$"); const out = []; const re = /\b([A-Z]{2,4})\s*:\s*\$\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/g; let m; while ((m = re.exec(text))) out.push({ t: m[1], p: parseFloat(m[2]) }); return out; } async function fetchTab() { const r = await fetch("https://www.espn.com.au/nba/schedule", { headers: TAB_HEADERS }); if (!r.ok) throw new Error("ESPN AU returned " + r.status); return parseTab(await r.text()); } async function applyTab(db) { if (!db.tabAt || Date.now() - db.tabAt > 30 * 60e3) { db.tabAt = Date.now(); try { const l = await fetchTab(); if (l.length) db.tabList = l; } catch (e) {} } const L = db.tabList || []; for (const g of upcoming(db)) { for (let i = 0; i < L.length - 1; i++) { const a = L[i], b = L[i + 1]; if (!((a.t === g.home && b.t === g.away) || (a.t === g.away && b.t === g.home))) continue; const price = (t) => (a.t === t ? a.p : b.p); const h = price(g.home), aw = price(g.away); g.tab = { h, a: aw, underdog: h === aw ? g.underdog : h > aw ? g.home : g.away }; g.underdog = g.tab.underdog; break; } } } function slateOf(db) { const u = upcoming(db); const date = u[0]?.date; const games = date ? Object.values(db.games).filter((g) => g.date === date).sort((a, b) => a.start.localeCompare(b.start)) : []; // most recent earlier day that has started games, for results const past = Object.values(db.games).filter((g) => Date.parse(g.start) <= Date.now() && g.date !== date).map((g) => g.date).sort().pop(); const recent = past ? Object.values(db.games).filter((g) => g.date === past).sort((a, b) => a.start.localeCompare(b.start)) : []; return { date, games, recent }; } function leaderboard(db) { const t = Object.fromEntries(players().map((n) => [n, { name: n, wins: 0, played: 0 }])); for (const g of Object.values(db.games)) { if (!g.winner) continue; for (const n of players()) { const team = (db.picks[g.id] || {})[n] || g.underdog; // no pick = underdog if (!team) continue; t[n].played++; if (team === g.winner) t[n].wins++; } } return Object.values(t).sort((a, b) => b.wins - a.wins || a.played - b.played); } async function nightly(env, force) { const db = await getDb(env); await refresh(db); await ensureUpcoming(db); await applySportsbet(db); await applyTab(db); db.checked = Date.now(); db.notified ||= {}; const { date, games } = slateOf(db); const lb = leaderboard(db); const lead = lb[0]?.played ? ` ${lb[0].name} leads on ${lb[0].wins}.` : ""; const out = {}; if (games.length && (force || !db.notified[date])) { const day = new Date(games[0].start).toLocaleDateString("en-AU", { weekday: "long", timeZone: "Australia/Melbourne" }); const only = CONFIG.NOTIFY_ONLY.split(",").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean); for (const n of Object.keys(db.subs)) { if (only.length && !only.includes(n)) continue; out[n] = await push(env, db, n, { title: "NBA picks 🏀", body: `${games.length} game${games.length > 1 ? "s" : ""} on ${day}.${lead} Tap to pick.`, url: "/" }); } if (!only.length) db.notified[date] = true; } await saveDb(env, db); return { slate: date, games: games.length, sent: out, oddsCreditsLeft: db.sbLeft ?? null }; } // ---------- HTTP ---------- const file = (body, type) => new Response(body, { headers: { "content-type": type, "cache-control": "no-cache" } }); const mp3 = (s) => file(Uint8Array.from(atob(s), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0)), "audio/mpeg"); const png = (s) => file(Uint8Array.from(atob(s), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0)), "image/png"); export default { async scheduled(_e, env, ctx) { ctx.waitUntil(nightly(env, false)); }, async fetch(req, env) { const url = new URL(req.url), p = url.pathname; const admin = url.searchParams.get("key") === CONFIG.ADMIN_KEY && CONFIG.ADMIN_KEY !== "change-this-password"; if (p === "/") return file(ASSETS.html, "text/html;charset=utf-8"); if (p === "/sw.js") return file(ASSETS.sw, "application/javascript"); if (p === "/manifest.json") return file(ASSETS.manifest, "application/manifest+json"); if (p === "/icon-192.png") return png(ASSETS.i192); if (p === "/icon-512.png") return png(ASSETS.i512); if (p === "/theme.mp3") return mp3(ASSETS.theme); if (p === "/api/state") { const db = await getDb(env); if (!db.checked || Date.now() - db.checked > 5 * 60e3) { await refresh(db); await ensureUpcoming(db); await applySportsbet(db); await applyTab(db); db.checked = Date.now(); await saveDb(env, db); } const { games, recent } = slateOf(db); const withPicks = (g) => ({ ...g, picks: db.picks[g.id] || {} }); return json({ players: players(), vapid: (await getVapid(env)).pub, games: games.map(withPicks), recent: recent.map(withPicks), board: leaderboard(db), subscribed: Object.keys(db.subs) }); } if (p === "/api/subscribe" && req.method === "POST") { const { name, sub } = await req.json(); if (!players().includes(name)) return json({ error: "unknown player" }, 400); const db = await getDb(env); db.subs[name] = sub; await saveDb(env, db); return json({ ok: true }); } if (p === "/api/pick" && req.method === "POST") { const { name, gameId, team } = await req.json(); const db = await getDb(env); const g = db.games[gameId]; if (!players().includes(name) || !g || ![g.home, g.away].includes(team)) return json({ error: "bad pick" }, 400); if (Date.now() >= Date.parse(g.start)) return json({ error: "game has started" }, 409); (db.picks[gameId] ||= {})[name] = team; await saveDb(env, db); return json({ ok: true }); } if (p === "/api/test" && admin) { const db = await getDb(env); const name = url.searchParams.get("name"); const s = await push(env, db, name, { title: "NBA picks 🏀", body: "Test notification. Tap to open.", url: "/" }); await saveDb(env, db); return json({ name, status: s }); } // Admin: load a specific US (Eastern) date's games, e.g. /api/load?key=..&date=20261021 if (p === "/api/load" && admin) { const db = await getDb(env); const date = url.searchParams.get("date"); const gs = await fetchGames(date); for (const g of gs) put(db, g); await applySportsbet(db); await applyTab(db); db.checked = Date.now(); await saveDb(env, db); return json({ date, games: gs.map((g) => `${g.away} @ ${g.home}`) }); } // Admin: check what the TAB prices scrape sees if (p === "/api/tab" && admin) { try { const r = await fetch("https://www.espn.com.au/nba/schedule", { headers: TAB_HEADERS }); const html = await r.text(); const l = parseTab(html); const i = html.search(/tab\.com\.au|ODDS BY|tabBetting/i); return json({ found: l.length, sample: l.slice(0, 8), status: r.status, bytes: html.length, url: r.url, dollars: (html.match(/\$/g) || []).length, tabMentions: (html.match(/tab\.com\.au/gi) || []).length, title: (html.match(/([^<]*)/i) || [])[1], around: i < 0 ? null : html.slice(Math.max(0, i - 150), i + 450) }); } catch (e) { return json({ error: String(e) }, 502); } } if (p === "/api/nightly" && admin) return json(await nightly(env, url.searchParams.get("force") === "1")); return json({ error: "not found" }, 404); }, };
